// V19 · A slide either takes the ball (won or deflected), catches the player first (foul) or misses: the defender is then on the ground.
function slideContact(d){const a=action,c=d.challenge;if(c?.kind!=='slide'||a.ended||c.done)return false;
 // Lot 3 · From behind, the legs are taken before the ball: any contact with the player is a foul, and the slide stops at his body.
 {const h=a.hero,hv=Math.hypot(h.vx||0,h.vz||0),fx=hv>.3?(h.vx||0)/hv:-Math.sin(h.angle||0),fz=hv>.3?(h.vz||0)/hv:-Math.cos(h.angle||0),behind=c.dx*fx+c.dz*fz>.45;if(behind&&c.time>.04&&c.time<.6&&Math.hypot(d.x+c.dx*.45-h.x,d.z+c.dz*.45-h.z)<.95){c.done=true;commitFoul(d);return true;}}
 if(challengeHits(d,a.ball)){c.done=true;if(Math.random()<.6)winBall(d,'Tacle glissé propre : il t’a pris le ballon.');else{Object.assign(a.ball,{vx:c.dx*6+(Math.random()-.5)*4,vy:.4,vz:c.dz*6+(Math.random()-.5)*4});makeLoose('Tacle glissé ! Le ballon file, va le chercher.',d);}return true;}
 if(c.time>.06&&c.time<.5&&Math.hypot(d.x+c.dx*.5-a.hero.x,d.z+c.dz*.5-a.hero.z)<.62){c.done=true;commitFoul(d);return true;}return false;}
// Foul: free kick (taken where it happened) or penalty in the box; a card for the defender, a red one when he was the last man.
function commitFoul(d){const a=action,h=a.hero,inBox=Math.abs(h.x)<20.16&&h.z<-3.5&&h.z>-20,lastMan=!a.defenders.some(q=>q!==d&&q.z<h.z-.5&&Math.abs(q.x-h.x)<14),key=d.tacticalId??'d'+a.defenders.indexOf(d);
 match.cards=match.cards||{};const prev=match.cards[key]||0;let card=null;if(lastMan&&!inBox||Math.random()<.06)card='red';else if(Math.random()<(d.style==='engagé'?.5:.35))card=prev?'red':'yellow';if(card)match.cards[key]=card==='red'?2:prev+1;
 match.foulsWon=(match.foulsWon||0)+1;if(card==='yellow')match.yellows=(match.yellows||0)+1;if(card==='red')match.reds=(match.reds||0)+1;
 for(const q of a.defenders){q.challenge=null;q.shoulder=false;q.lean=0;}h.lean=0;
 // Lot 2 · The referee runs to the culprit and shows the card: a short close-up before the free kick.
 if(card){const side=d.x>0?-1:1,c={d,card,inBox,t:0,rx:d.x+side*7,rz:d.z+5,rangle:0,rspeed:0,raised:false,name:d.name||'Le défenseur'};a.cardShow=c;a.phase='card';a.ready=false;a.flying=false;a.loose=false;a.runTarget=null;Object.assign(a.ball,{vx:0,vy:0,vz:0});ambience.whistle?.(2);$('feedback').textContent='Coup de sifflet… l’arbitre arrive.';d.speed=0;h.speed=0;return;}
 finishFoul(d,card,inBox);}
function tickCard(a,dt){const c=a.cardShow,d=c.d;c.t+=dt;a.frozen=true;const spot={x:d.x+(c.rx<d.x?-1.3:1.3),z:d.z+.6},dx=spot.x-c.rx,dz=spot.z-c.rz,l=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
 if(l>.05&&c.t<1.6){const v=Math.min(l,5.2*dt);c.rx+=dx/l*v;c.rz+=dz/l*v;c.rspeed=5.2;c.rangle=Math.atan2(-dx,-dz);}else{c.rspeed=0;c.rangle=Math.atan2(-(d.x-c.rx),-(d.z-c.rz));}
 d.angle=Math.atan2(-(c.rx-d.x),-(c.rz-d.z));
 if(!c.raised&&c.t>1.9){c.raised=true;announce(c.card==='red'?'🟥 CARTON ROUGE':'🟨 CARTON JAUNE',c.name+(c.card==='red'?' est expulsé':' est averti'),1.8);say(c.card==='red'?'red':'foul',{team:'L’adversaire'});}
 if(c.t>3.6){a.cardShow=null;finishFoul(d,c.card,c.inBox,true);}}
function finishFoul(d,card,inBox,shown=false){const a=action,h=a.hero;
 if(card==='red'){a.defenders.splice(a.defenders.indexOf(d),1);const p=match.tactical?.players.find(p=>p.id===d.tacticalId);if(p)p.out=true;}
 a.ball={x:h.x,y:R,z:h.z,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,age:0,bounces:0};a.loose=false;a.flying=false;a.released=false;a.runTarget=null;
 if(inBox)setupPenalty();else setupFreeKick(30,14);a.chanceKind=inBox?'penalty':'freekick';a.phase='ready';a.ready=true;a.started=true;h.speed=0;h.kickTimer=-1;a.pressIndex=nearestDefender(h);engine.setup(a);engine.resize();
 $('kind').textContent=inBox?'PENALTY':'COUP FRANC';if(!shown){announce('FAUTE',(d.name||'Le défenseur')+' te fauche · '+(inBox?'penalty !':'coup franc'),2.4);say('foul',{team:'L’adversaire'});}}
// V19 · Offside: judged when the pass is played. A team-mate (or the player himself on a return pass) in the opposing
// half, ahead of the ball and beyond the second-last defender (the keeper counts) is offside if he then plays the ball.
function offsideLineZ(a){const zs=[a.keeper,...a.defenders].filter(Boolean).map(p=>p.z).sort((p,q)=>p-q);return zs[1]??GOAL_Z_LINE;}
const GOAL_Z_LINE=-20,HALFWAY=(PITCH.far-20)/2;
function offsideAtPass(passer){const a=action;if(match.training)return null;const line=offsideLineZ(a),ballZ=a.ball.z,players=[a.hero,...a.teammates].filter(p=>p&&p!==passer&&p.z<HALFWAY&&p.z<ballZ-.3&&p.z<line-.25);return players.length?{players,line}:null;}
const isOffsidePlayer=p=>!!action.offside?.players.includes(p);
function callOffside(p){const a=action;a.flag=true;match.offsides=(match.offsides||0)+1;const d=[...a.defenders,a.keeper].filter(Boolean).reduce((b,q)=>Math.hypot(q.x-a.ball.x,q.z-a.ball.z)<Math.hypot(b.x-a.ball.x,b.z-a.ball.z)?q:b);a.newOwner=d.tacticalId!=null?d:a.keeper;a.escapeTarget={x:a.newOwner.x,z:a.newOwner.z+1.5};ambience.whistle(1);say('offside',{who:p===a.hero?career.name:p.name||'ton partenaire'});resolve(false,(p===a.hero?'Hors-jeu ! Tu étais parti trop tôt.':'Hors-jeu de '+(p.name||'ton partenaire')+' !')+' Le drapeau se lève : coup franc adverse.',a.shotBy,'offside');}
function deflectBall(defender){const c=defender.challenge,ball=action.ball,dot=ball.vx*c.dx+ball.vz*c.dz;ball.vx-=1.6*dot*c.dx;ball.vz-=1.6*dot*c.dz;ball.vy=Math.max(ball.vy,1.6);makeLoose('Contré ! Le ballon reste en jeu : cours vers lui.',defender);}
function makeLoose(message,ignore=null){action.offside=null;action.incomingPass=false;action.ball.spin=0;action.runTarget=null;action.combination=false;action.followTarget=null;action.loose=true;action.flying=true;action.phase='flight';action.ballControl='loose';action.outcomeKind='deflection';action.contactIgnore=ignore;action.ignoreUntil=action.sim+.22;action.ball.age=0;action.looseTime=0;$('feedback').textContent=message;}
function winBall(defender,message){defender.challenge=null;resolve(false,message,action.shotBy,'tackle');action.newOwner=defender;action.ballControl='defender';defender.kickTimer=.18;action.escapeTarget={x:clamp(defender.x+(defender.x<action.hero.x?-1:1),-PITCH.W+1,PITCH.W-1),z:defender.z+2};defender.angle=Math.atan2(-(action.escapeTarget.x-defender.x),-(action.escapeTarget.z-defender.z));attachBall(defender);}
function patrol(p,i,dt){const t=action.sim;moveToward(p,{x:p.home.x+Math.sin(t*.85+i)*.6,z:p.home.z+Math.sin(t*.65+i)*.4},.75,dt);}
function updateActors(a,dt){if(a.phase==='press')return;
 for(const p of [a.hero,...(a.teammates||[a.receiver]),...a.defenders]){if(p)p.previous={x:p.x,z:p.z};if(p&&p.kickTimer>=0)p.kickTimer+=dt;}
 const carrier=['choice','offball','teammateRun'].includes(a.phase)?a.receiver:a.phase==='windup'?a.windup.player:a.hero;
 if(!a.ended){a.defenceClock=(a.defenceClock||0)-dt;if(a.defenceClock<=0){a.pressIndex=refreshDefence(a.defenders,carrier,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.pressIndex);a.defenceClock=.18;}}
 const supporter=a.phase==='shield'?(a.teammates||[]).filter(t=>t.tacticalId%11!==0).reduce((b,t)=>!b||Math.hypot(t.x-a.hero.x,t.z-a.hero.z)<Math.hypot(b.x-a.hero.x,b.z-a.hero.z)?t:b,null):null;
 const looseChase=a.flying&&a.loose?[a.defenders,a.shotBy==='push'?[]:a.teammates].map(group=>group.reduce((best,p)=>!best||Math.hypot(p.x-a.ball.x,p.z-a.ball.z)<Math.hypot(best.x-a.ball.x,best.z-a.ball.z)?p:best,null)):[null,null];
 for(let i=0;i<a.defenders.length;i++){
  const d=a.defenders[i];d.y=0;if(!a.ended&&d.hold&&!a.released){d.speed=0;continue;}if(!a.ended&&d.wallUntil&&a.sim<d.wallUntil){const t=a.sim-d.jumpAt;d.y=t>0&&t<.5?.3*Math.sin(Math.PI*t/.5):0;d.speed=0;continue;}if(a.ended){if(d.challenge)updateChallenge(d,dt);if(d===a.newOwner&&a.after<1.1)moveToward(d,a.escapeTarget,2.7,dt);else d.speed=0;continue;}
  if(updateChallenge(d,dt))continue;const distance=Math.hypot(d.x-a.ball.x,d.z-a.ball.z);
  if(d===looseChase[0]&&a.ball.y<1.6&&distance>1){moveToward(d,{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.18,z:a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.18},4.3*match.profile.press,dt,10);continue;}
  if((a.phase==='dribble'||a.phase==='skill')&&(i===a.pressIndex||distance<match.profile.reach*.8)&&distance<match.profile.reach*(DEF_STYLE[d.style]?.reach||1)){if(beginChallenge(d,{x:a.ball.x+(a.hero.vx||0)*.3,z:a.ball.z+(a.hero.vz||0)*.3},'poke'))continue;/* Lot 2 · the stab goes where the ball will be, not where it was */}
  // V19 · Slide tackle from 1.4–3.4 m, aimed where the ball is going. Committed defenders try it far more often.
  if((a.phase==='dribble'||a.phase==='skill')&&!d.challenge&&!(d.challengeCooldown>0)&&distance>1.4&&distance<3.4){const hv=Math.hypot(a.hero.vx||0,a.hero.vz||0),fromBehind=hv>.5&&((a.hero.x-d.x)*(a.hero.vx||0)+(a.hero.z-d.z)*(a.hero.vz||0))/(hv*distance)>.5,rate=(d.style==='engagé'?.55:d.style==='anticipe'?.28:.12)*match.profile.press*(fromBehind?.25:1);if(Math.random()<dt*rate&&beginChallenge(d,{x:a.ball.x+(a.hero.vx||0)*.3,y:R,z:a.ball.z+(a.hero.vz||0)*.3},'slide'))continue;}
  // V19 · Shoulder duel: a defender who catches the carrier from behind or the side runs with him, shoulder to shoulder.
  d.shoulder=false;d.lean=0;if(a.phase==='dribble'&&!d.challenge){const h=a.hero,sx=d.x-h.x,sz=d.z-h.z,sd=Math.hypot(sx,sz)||1,gx=-h.x,gz=-20-h.z,gl=Math.hypot(gx,gz)||1;if(sd<1.25&&(sx*gx+sz*gz)/(sd*gl)<.35){d.shoulder=true;const side=Math.sign(sx*gz-sz*gx)||1;d.lean=-side*.22;moveToward(d,{x:h.x+sx/sd*.78+(h.vx||0)*.12,z:h.z+sz/sd*.78+(h.vz||0)*.12},4.9,dt,14);continue;}}
  if(a.flying&&!a.incomingPass&&distance<1.6&&a.ball.y<1.65){if(beginChallenge(d,a.ball,'block'))continue;}
  const intent=a.phase==='dribble'?a.dribbleEnd:a.phase==='skill'?a.skill.end:a.phase==='teammateRun'?a.teamTarget:carrier;
  if(d.stun>0){d.stun-=dt;d.speed=0;continue;}const st=DEF_STYLE[d.style]||DEF_STYLE.prudent,lead=intent&&intent!==carrier?{x:carrier.x+(intent.x-carrier.x)*st.lead,z:carrier.z+(intent.z-carrier.z)*st.lead}:intent;
  let target=defensiveTarget(d,carrier,a.ball,i===a.pressIndex,a.flying,lead||carrier);
  // Lot 4 · On a cross to the player his marker contests from the goal side, shoulder to shoulder, instead of running through him to the ball.
  if(a.phase==='incoming'&&a.incomingMode==='lob'&&i===a.pressIndex){const h=a.hero,sd=Math.sign(d.x-h.x)||1;target={x:h.x+sd*.75,z:h.z-.55};}if(i===a.pressIndex&&carrier===a.hero)target.x+=(match.sideBias||0)*.7;
  const beaten=carrier&&d.z>carrier.z+.5&&Math.hypot(d.x-carrier.x,d.z-carrier.z)<9?1.2:1;/* Lot 2 · a beaten defender sprints back */moveToward(d,target,(i===a.pressIndex?4.35:3.8)*st.speed*beaten*match.profile.press*(a.phase==='windup'?.55:1),dt,9);
 }
 for(const t of a.teammates||[]){if(a.ended){celebrate(a,t,dt);continue;}if(t===carrier)continue;if(t.hold&&!a.released){t.speed=0;continue;}
  if(a.throughRun?.mate===t&&(a.phase==='windup'||a.flying&&!a.loose)){const tr=a.throughRun;if(!tr.attack&&a.flying&&Math.hypot(t.x-tr.point.x,t.z-tr.point.z)<1.2)tr.attack=true;/* at the spot he attacks the ball */moveToward(t,tr.attack?{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.15,z:a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.15}:tr.point,5.1,dt,10);continue;}
  if(a.depthRun?.mate===t&&(a.phase==='windup'||a.flying||a.phase==='choice')){moveToward(t,a.depthRun.point,4.8,dt,10);continue;}
  {const run=a.runs?.find(r=>r.mate===t);if(run&&!a.loose&&['dribble','skill','windup','flight'].includes(a.phase)&&t!==a.passRecipient){moveToward(t,run.point,4.7,dt,10);continue;}}
  if(a.phase==='shield'&&t===supporter){const dx=t.x-a.hero.x,dz=t.z-a.hero.z,l=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;moveToward(t,{x:a.hero.x+dx/l*4.5,z:a.hero.z+dz/l*4.5},4.6,dt,10);continue;}if(t===looseChase[1]&&a.ball.y<1.8&&Math.hypot(t.x-a.ball.x,t.z-a.ball.z)<14){moveToward(t,{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.18,z:a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.18},4.5*fatigueFactor(100),dt,10);continue;}if(t===a.passRecipient&&(a.flying||a.phase==='windup')){const target=a.flying?a.flightTarget:a.windup.target;moveToward(t,target,3.6,dt,8);continue;}
  const nearest=a.defenders.reduce((best,d)=>!best||Math.hypot(d.x-t.x,d.z-t.z)<Math.hypot(best.x-t.x,best.z-t.z)?d:best,null);
  // Offer width and depth; move away from a close marker instead of wandering.
  const away=nearest&&Math.hypot(nearest.x-t.x,nearest.z-t.z)<2?Math.sign(t.x-nearest.x)||1:0;
  // Possession: partners come closer to offer short options. Counter-attack: they sprint forward.
  const tt=teamTactic(),target={x:clamp(t.home.x+away*1.7+(carrier.x-t.home.x)*(tt==='possession'?.22:.08),-PITCH.W+1.5,PITCH.W-1.5),z:clamp(t.home.z+(t.support?-.5:-1.4)+clamp((carrier.z-a.start.z)*.65,-8,8)-(tt==='contre'&&!t.support?1.5:0),-18,PITCH.far-2)};
  moveToward(t,target,2.7*(tt==='contre'?1.2:1),dt,8);
 }

 // No two character bodies may occupy the same space, including after a tackle.
 for(let pass=0;pass<2;pass++){
  for(const d of a.defenders){separateBodies(d,a.hero,.82,true);for(const t of a.teammates||[a.receiver])if(t)separateBodies(d,t,.82,true);}
  for(let i=0;i<a.defenders.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<a.defenders.length;j++)separateBodies(a.defenders[i],a.defenders[j],.82);
 }

 if(a.ended&&a.celebration)celebrate(a,a.hero,dt);
 else if(a.flying&&a.loose)moveToward(a.hero,{x:a.ball.x+a.ball.vx*.15,z:clamp(a.ball.z+a.ball.vz*.15,-19.3,PITCH.far-1)},attributes(developmentXP()).speed*fatigueFactor(match.energy)*1.15,dt);
 else if(a.runTarget&&(a.phase==='offball'||a.flying&&a.shotBy!=='return'))moveToward(a.hero,a.runTarget,4.5*fatigueFactor(match.energy),dt);
 else if(a.combination&&['windup','flight'].includes(a.phase)&&a.shotBy==='return')moveToward(a.hero,a.returnSpot,4.6*fatigueFactor(match.energy),dt);
 else if(a.flying&&a.passRecipient===a.hero&&a.shotBy==='teamPass'&&a.phase==='flight')moveToward(a.hero,{x:a.ball.x,z:a.ball.z},2.2*fatigueFactor(match.energy),dt);
 else if(a.flying&&a.shotBy==='hero'&&a.followTarget)moveToward(a.hero,a.followTarget,1.35,dt);
 else if(a.phase!=='dribble')a.hero.speed=0;
}
function resolveBodyContacts(a){if(a.phase!=='windup'&&a.hero.y>0)a.hero.y=Math.max(0,a.hero.y-.02);const team=a.teammates||[];for(let i=0;i<team.length;i++){separateBodies(team[i],a.hero,.82,true);for(let j=i+1;j<team.length;j++)separateBodies(team[i],team[j],.82);}for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){for(const d of a.defenders){separateBodies(d,a.hero,.82,true);for(const t of a.teammates||[a.receiver])if(t)separateBodies(d,t,.82,true);}for(let i=0;i<a.defenders.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<a.defenders.length;j++)separateBodies(a.defenders[i],a.defenders[j],.82);}}
function tick(dt){if(!action||guideOpen)return;const a=action;if(a.phase==='interval'){tickInterval(dt);return;}if(a.phase==='card'){tickCard(a,dt);return;}if(a.phase==='incoming'){a.incomingTime+=dt;a.incomingPlayer.kickTimer+=dt;if(a.incomingTime>=.32){if(!a.incomingReleased){a.ball=launch(a.ball.vx===0&&a.ball.x!==undefined&&a.incomingMode?a.ball:a.incomingPlayer,a.incomingTarget,a.incomingMode||'low');a.incomingReleased=true;a.kicker=a.incomingPlayer;a.shotBy='teamPass';a.passRecipient=a.hero;
  // Lot 1 · A ground pass is a real pass: received, cut out, left loose or out, like any other ball.
  if(a.incomingMode!=='lob'){handOffIncoming();return;}}a.sim+=dt;a.flying=true;updateActors(a,dt);stepBall(a.ball,dt);if(a.incomingMode==='lob'&&a.incomingTarget.z<-15.5&&Math.abs(a.incomingTarget.x)<5.5){const k=a.keeper,tk=Math.hypot(k.x-a.incomingTarget.x,k.z-a.incomingTarget.z),th=Math.hypot(a.hero.x-a.incomingTarget.x,a.hero.z-a.incomingTarget.z);if(tk<th-.6&&a.ball.y<2.6&&a.ball.vy<0&&Math.hypot(a.ball.x-k.x,a.ball.z-k.z)<1.6){resolve(false,'Le gardien sort et capte le centre.',a.shotBy,'save');a.ballControl='held';k.holding=true;return;}}
  const reach=Math.hypot(a.ball.x-a.hero.x,a.ball.z-a.hero.z);
  if(a.incomingMode==='lob'&&reach<1.2&&a.ball.y>.75&&a.ball.y<2.6&&a.ball.vy<0){if(!aerialDuel())beginAerial();return;}
  if(a.ball.y<.75&&hitPlayer(a.ball,a.hero,.65,1.5)){beginAerial();return;}
  for(const d of a.defenders)if(a.ball.vy<0&&!d.crossTried&&hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.42,2.2)&&Math.hypot(a.ball.x-d.x,a.ball.z-d.z)<reach-.5){d.crossTried=true;if(Math.random()<.35){clearCross(d);return;}}
  // Once the cross has bounced it is an ordinary ball: no more automatic control.
  if(a.ball.bounces>0||a.ball.age>4)handOffIncoming();}return;}if(a.callPause){a.callWait=(a.callWait||0)+dt;if(a.callWait>=3){a.callPause=false;a.callWait=0;}}
 // V19 · Freeze frame: during a decision every player keeps his posture and momentum, like a paused video.
 if(!a.ready)a.runsPlanned=false;else if(!a.runsPlanned){a.runsPlanned=true;planRuns(a);}
 if((a.ready||a.callPause||a.phase==='choice'&&match.training||!a.started)&&!a.ended){a.frozen=true;return;}a.frozen=false;a.sim+=dt;drainEnergy((a.hero.speed||0)*dt*.09);updateActors(a,dt);
 if(!match.training){const k=a.keeper;if(k.state==='set'&&!a.flying&&!k.holding){const h=a.hero,dist=Math.hypot(h.x,h.z+20),alone=!a.defenders.some(d=>d.z<h.z&&Math.abs(d.x-h.x)<2.5&&d.z>-20);const want=-19.25+(alone&&dist<13?clamp((13-dist)/6,0,1)*1.9:0);k.rush=false;if(alone&&dist<17&&['dribble','skill'].includes(a.phase))keeperRush(k,dt);else k.z+=clamp(want-k.z,-2.5*dt,2.5*dt);}updateKeeper(k,a.ball,dt,a.flying&&a.shotBy!=='return');if(!a.ended&&k.rush&&keeperSmother(k))return;}
 if(a.hero.grounded&&a.sim>a.hero.grounded){a.hero.grounded=0;a.hero.bicycle=false;}
 if(!match.training)a.offsideLine=offsideLineZ(a);
 if(a.ready){attachBall(a.hero);}
 else if(a.phase==='dribble'){a.dribbleElapsed+=dt;const held=a.defenders.find(d=>d.shoulder&&Math.hypot(a.hero.x-d.x,a.hero.z-d.z)<1.1);a.hero.lean=held?-held.lean:0;/* Lot 4 · the carrier runs flat out through his touch (no braking before the end of it), as fast as a defender of the same level */const reached=moveToward(a.hero,a.dribbleEnd,attributes(developmentXP()).speed*fatigueFactor(match.energy)*(held?.86:1),dt,14,false);attachBall(a.hero,true);
  if(held&&Math.random()<dt*.5*match.profile.press*(held.style==='engagé'?1.3:1)){const b=a.ball;b.vx=(a.hero.vx||0)*.7+(a.hero.x-held.x)*2;b.vz=(a.hero.vz||0)*.7+(a.hero.z-held.z)*2;a.kicker=a.hero;makeLoose('Duel d’épaule perdu : le ballon t’échappe !',a.hero);}
  else for(const d of a.defenders){if(slideContact(d))break;if(d.challenge?.kind==='slide')continue;const close=Math.hypot(a.hero.x-d.x,a.hero.z-d.z);if(challengeHits(d,a.ball)||Math.hypot(a.ball.x-d.x,a.ball.z-d.z)<.59||close<.95&&!d.shoulder&&Math.random()<dt*1.5*match.profile.press){winBall(d,'Ballon récupéré par le défenseur. Essaie de le contourner.');break;}}if(!a.ended&&(reached||a.dribbleElapsed>1.7))controlAgain('Dribble terminé · pause. Choisis la prochaine touche ou prépare ta frappe.');updateDistance();}
 else if(a.phase==='skill'){
  const k=a.skill;k.time+=dt;a.hero.skillTimer=k.time;const t=Math.min(1,k.time/k.duration),smooth=t*t*(3-2*t);a.hero.x=k.start.x+(k.end.x-k.start.x)*smooth;a.hero.z=k.start.z+(k.end.z-k.start.z)*smooth;a.hero.speed=2.5;
  a.ball={x:k.ballStart.x+(k.end.x-k.ballStart.x)*smooth,y:R,z:k.ballStart.z+(k.end.z-.58-k.ballStart.z)*smooth,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,age:0,bounces:0};
  for(const d of a.defenders){if(slideContact(d))break;if(d.challenge?.kind==='slide')continue;if(challengeHits(d,a.ball)||hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.35,.8)){winBall(d,'Le défenseur a lu le râteau. Essaie l’autre côté ou un autre timing.');break;}}
  // A completed rake wrong-foots the defenders who were close: they need a moment to turn.
  if(!a.ended&&t>=1){for(const d of a.defenders)if(Math.hypot(d.x-a.hero.x,d.z-a.hero.z)<3.2)d.stun=Math.max(d.stun||0,.45);controlAgain('Râteau terminé · pause. À toi de choisir la suite.');}updateDistance();
 }
 else if(a.phase==='press'){tickPress(a,dt);}
 else if(a.phase==='shield'){a.shieldTime+=dt;const d=a.defenders[a.pressIndex];if(d){a.hero.angle=Math.atan2(-(a.hero.x-d.x),-(a.hero.z-d.z));if(Math.hypot(d.x-a.hero.x,d.z-a.hero.z)<1&&Math.random()<dt*.35){winBall(d,'Le défenseur passe devant et récupère le ballon.');return;}}a.hero.speed=0;attachBall(a.hero);if(a.shieldTime>=1.3)controlAgain('Ballon protégé. Ton partenaire est venu en soutien.');}
 else if(a.phase==='feint'){a.feintTime+=dt;a.hero.speed=0;attachBall(a.hero);if(a.feintTime>=.42)controlAgain($('feedback').textContent);}
 else if(a.phase==='windup'){a.windup.elapsed+=dt;if(a.aerial){const ae=a.aerial;if(ae.bicycle){a.hero.y=ae.jump+.55*Math.sin(Math.min(1,a.windup.elapsed/.3)*Math.PI/2);if(a.windup.elapsed>=.3)releaseBall();}else{if(ae.header)a.hero.y=ae.jump;if(a.windup.elapsed>=.12)releaseBall();}}else{attachBall(a.windup.player);if(a.windup.elapsed>=.28)releaseBall();}}
 else if(a.phase==='choice'){attachBall(a.receiver);a.decisionTime=(a.decisionTime||0)+dt;if(a.decisionTime>.4)playTeamDecision(teamDecision(a.receiver,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.defenders,a.keeper,a.runTarget?a.hero:null));}
 else if(a.phase==='offball'){a.runTime+=dt;attachBall(a.receiver);for(const d of a.defenders){if(hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.35,1.4)){winBall(d,'Le défenseur a coupé la remise.');break;}}if(!a.ended&&(Math.hypot(a.hero.x-a.runTarget.x,a.hero.z-a.runTarget.z)<.8||a.runTime>1.1)){a.returnSpot={...a.runTarget};const pick=teamDecision(a.receiver,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.defenders,a.keeper,a.hero);/* Lot 1 · The call makes the run more attractive; the partner still weighs every option. */if(pick.kind==='pass'&&pick.receiver===a.hero&&laneClearance(a.receiver,a.returnSpot,a.defenders)>.85){windUp(a.receiver,a.returnSpot,'low','return');}else{a.runTarget=null;a.combination=false;playTeamDecision(pick);if(pick.kind!=='shot')$('feedback').textContent=pick.kind==='carry'?'Ton partenaire a vu ton appel, mais il garde le ballon.':'Ton partenaire a vu ton appel, mais il choisit une autre solution.';}}}
 else if(a.phase==='teammateRun'){a.carryTime=(a.carryTime||0)+dt;const arrived=moveToward(a.receiver,a.teamTarget,3.4,dt);attachBall(a.receiver,true);for(const d of a.defenders)if(hitPlayer(a.ball,d,.38,1.5)){winBall(d,'Le défenseur récupère le ballon.');break;}if(!a.ended&&(arrived||a.carryTime>1.1))playTeamDecision(teamDecision(a.receiver,[a.hero,...a.teammates],a.defenders,a.keeper,a.runTarget?a.hero:null));}
 else if(a.flying){a.flightTime+=dt;const before=stepBall(a.ball,dt);
  const contacts=[];for(const p of [...a.defenders,...a.teammates,a.hero]){if(p===a.kicker&&!a.loose||p===a.contactIgnore&&a.sim<a.ignoreUntil)continue;/* Lot 3 · the pass that opens the chance always reaches the player */if(a.incomingPass&&p!==a.hero)continue;const opponent=a.defenders.includes(p);const contact=sweptPlayerContact(before,a.ball,p,opponent?.38:.65,(opponent?1.7:1.55)+(p.y||0));if(contact)contacts.push({p,opponent,contact});}
  contacts.sort((x,y)=>x.contact.t-y.contact.t);const touch=contacts[0];
  if(touch){const {p,opponent,contact}=touch;Object.assign(a.ball,{x:contact.x,y:contact.y,z:contact.z});if(!opponent&&isOffsidePlayer(p)){callOffside(p);return;}if(opponent){if(challengeHits(p,a.ball)&&p.challenge?.kind==='block')deflectBall(p);else winBall(p,'Intercepté ! Le défenseur a fermé la trajectoire.');}else if(p===a.hero){if(a.incomingPass&&a.ball.y<.75){a.incomingPass=false;beginAerial();$('feedback').textContent='Bien reçu · contrôle orienté ou joue directement.';return;}controlAgain('Bien reçu · à toi de rejouer !');return;}else{a.receiver=p;receivePass();return;}}

  const frame=!a.ended&&frameContact(before,a.ball);if(frame){makeLoose(frame==='bar'?'Barre ! Suis le second ballon.':'Poteau ! Suis le second ballon.');say('frame');}
  if(!a.ended&&!(a.contactIgnore===a.keeper&&a.sim<a.ignoreUntil)){const contact=sweptKeeperTouch(before,a.ball,a.keeper);if(contact){Object.assign(a.ball,contact);/* Lot 4 · into his body he holds it; at full stretch only a soft ball is held, the rest is pushed away */if(Math.hypot(a.ball.vx,a.ball.vz)>(Math.abs(a.ball.x-a.keeper.x)<.55?25:17)){a.ball.vx=(a.ball.x-a.keeper.x)*4;a.ball.vz=Math.max(4,Math.abs(a.ball.vz)*.28);a.ball.vy=2;a.ball.z=a.keeper.z+.85;makeLoose('Le gardien repousse ! Le ballon est encore jouable.',a.keeper);}else resolve(false,'Arrêt du gardien ! Le ballon est bloqué dans ses mains.',a.shotBy,'save');}}
  if(!a.ended&&a.loose&&a.ball.y<1.5&&hitPlayer(a.ball,a.hero,.65,1.3)){controlAgain('Second ballon récupéré · pause. À toi de rejouer !');return;}
  if(!a.ended&&!a.loose&&Math.hypot(a.ball.vx,a.ball.vz)<.7&&a.flightTime>2)makeLoose('Ballon libre : tente de le récupérer.');
  const crossing=goalCrossing(before,a.ball);if(!a.ended&&!frame&&crossing){if(crossing.inside)resolve(true,a.shotBy==='teammate'?(a.goalAssist?'BUUUT ! Passe décisive pour toi.':'BUUUT ! Le mouvement collectif est récompensé.'):a.combination?'BUUUT ! Tu conclus le une-deux.':'BUUUT ! Le ballon finit dans les filets.');else resolve(false,crossing.y>2.44?'Au-dessus ! La trajectoire dépasse la barre.':'À côté ! Le ballon passe hors du cadre.');}
  if(!a.ended&&(Math.abs(a.ball.x)>PITCH.W+.5||a.ball.z>PITCH.far||a.ball.age>(a.loose?7:5)||a.ball.z<-24))resolve(false,a.shotBy==='return'?'La remise est interceptée ou trop longue.':'Occasion manquée. Ajuste la direction et la puissance.');
 }else if(a.ended){
  a.after+=dt;
  if(a.ballControl==='net')stepNetBall(a.ball,dt);
  else if(a.ballControl==='held')Object.assign(a.ball,keeperGrip(a.keeper),{vx:0,vy:0,vz:0});
  else if(a.ballControl==='defender')attachBall(a.newOwner,a.after<1.1);
  else if(a.ballControl==='loose'&&a.after<4)stepBall(a.ball,dt);
  if(a.after>.9){if(!match.training)$('continue').hidden=false;if(!replay&&a.tape?.length>=TAPE_HZ)$('replayBtn').hidden=false;}
 }
 resolveBodyContacts(a);
}
function loop(t){const dt=Math.min((t-lastTime)/1000,.04);lastTime=t;time+=dt;if(action&&!document.hidden&&replay)playReplay(dt);else if(action&&!document.hidden){if(drag&&(drag.kind==='ball'||drag.kind==='target'&&Date.now()-drag.began>=LOB_HOLD_MS))updateAim(drag.current);let remaining=dt;while(remaining>0){const d=Math.min(remaining,1/120);tick(d);remaining-=d;}if(!guideOpen)recordFrame(action,dt);if(action){if(action.phase==='interval')drawTactical($('tacticalPitch'),match.tactical);else engine.render(action,dt,time);}updateOffscreen();updateCoach();updateCrowd();updateReplayTimer();if(match?.commentAt!=null&&time-match.commentAt>3.4&&!$('commentary').hidden)$('commentary').hidden=true;engine.setRunCursor?.(action&&action.runTarget&&!action.ended&&action.phase!=='interval'?action.runTarget:null,!!action?.callPause);if(action?.ended&&!match?.training&&action.after>continueDelay(action)&&!action.autoNext){action.autoNext=true;$('continue').onclick();}}frameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}
async function init(){$('fullscreen').hidden=!$('match').requestFullscreen;refresh();setMode('normal');try{const {Pitch3D}=await import('./engine.js?v=20-4');engine=new Pitch3D(canvas,career);engine.setCrowdVisible($('crowdToggle').checked);$('engineStatus').textContent='Prêt à entrer sur le terrain · '+BUILD;if($('footBuild'))$('footBuild').textContent=BUILD+' · Entre sur le terrain';$('play').disabled=false;$('practice').disabled=false;frameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(loop);}catch(e){console.error(e);$('engineStatus').textContent='La 3D est indisponible. Essaie Safari ou Chrome avec WebGL activé.';$('play').disabled=true;}}
canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(action){action=null;match=null;screen('home');}$('play').disabled=true;$('engineStatus').textContent='Affichage 3D interrompu. Recharge la page pour reprendre.';});init();

$('exportSave').onclick=()=>{const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify({format:'next-nine',version:1,career},null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='next-nine-carriere.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);};
$('importSave').onclick=()=>$('saveFile').click();
$('saveFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{const file=e.target.files?.[0];if(!file)return;try{if(file.size>100000)throw Error();const data=JSON.parse(await file.text());if(data.format!=='next-nine'||data.version!==1)throw Error();const imported=parseCareer(data.career);if(confirm(`Remplacer ta carrière par celle de ${imported.name} (${imported.games} matchs) ?`)){career=imported;save();$('storage').textContent=storageOK?'Carrière importée et enregistrée.':'Carrière importée pour cette session. Le navigateur ne permet pas sa sauvegarde.';}}catch{$('storage').textContent='Fichier invalide : ta carrière actuelle est conservée.';}e.target.value='';});

===== dist/gestures.js =====
import {clamp} from './physics.js?v=20-4';
export const LOB_HOLD_MS=480;
export function targetGesture(elapsed,teammate=false){return elapsed>=LOB_HOLD_MS?'lob':teammate?'pass':'dribble';}

export function lateralSkill(points,elapsed){if(points.length<2||elapsed>650)return null;const a=points[0],b=points[points.length-1],dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;return Math.abs(dx)>=38&&Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)*1.8?{kind:'rake',side:Math.sign(dx)}:null;}

// Goal-centred slingshot: aim stays editable; horizontal movement never adds spin.
export function assistedShot(start,current){const dx=current.x-start.x,dy=current.y-start.y;return {target:{x:clamp(-dx/Math.max(45,dy)*5.5,-5.2,5.2),z:-21},power:clamp(.28+Math.max(0,dy)/140*.72,.28,1),active:dy>=18};}

 <div id="combination" class="combination arena-choice" hidden><p>Touche l’espace libre pendant la passe pour faire ton appel. Ton partenaire privilégie la frappe en bonne position, même si tu appelles le ballon.</p><button id="oneTwo" class="primary">Demander le une-deux ↗</button><button id="teammateShot" class="secondary">Le laisser tirer →</button></div>
 <aside id="gestureGuide" class="gesture-guide" hidden aria-label="Gestes du jeu"><div class="guide-card"><p class="eyebrow">PRENDS LE TERRAIN EN MAIN</p><h2>Tout se joue au doigt.</h2><dl><div><dt>Dribbler</dt><dd>Touche l’herbe là où tu veux avancer. Une touche courte de balle, puis tu rejoues.</dd></div><div><dt>Râteau</dt><dd>Pars de l’herbe, en dehors du ballon, et balaye rapidement le terrain vers la gauche ou la droite, à l’arrêt ou pendant la conduite. Le ballon passe sous le pied et tu changes d’appui.</dd></div><div><dt>Passer</dt><dd>Touche directement ton coéquipier bleu.</dd></div><div><dt>Lober</dt><dd>Maintiens le doigt sur la destination, attends l’indication « LOB », puis relâche.</dd></div><div><dt>Frapper</dt><dd>Depuis le ballon ou une zone libre du terrain, glisse vers toi. Déplace ton doigt de côté pour ajuster la cible : tirer à gauche vise à droite. Relâche pour frapper. La direction reste modifiable jusqu’au lâcher. La ligne courte indique seulement le départ du ballon. Une traction excessive fait monter la frappe et peut la faire passer au-dessus : la jauge n’affiche que le pourcentage, à toi de sentir le dosage.</dd></div><div><dt>Consigne du coach</dt><dd>Avant chaque match, le coach te donne une consigne adaptée à ton poste. Elle reste affichée avec ton compteur. Respectée : +35 XP et sa confiance monte. Cette confiance décide si tu es titulaire et quand tu sors.</dd></div><div><dt>Tactique d’équipe</dt><dd>Choisis-la avant le match ou change-la pendant la simulation (bouton TACTIQUE). Pressing haut : ballons récupérés haut, mais espace dans le dos et fatigue après l’heure de jeu. Contre-attaque : bloc bas et transitions rapides. Possession : passes courtes et moins de pertes. Elle change le match simulé et la place de tes partenaires pendant les occasions.</dd></div><div><dt>Titulaire ou remplaçant</dt><dd>Si la confiance du coach passe sous 40 % ou si tu arrives fatigué (moins de 45 % avant la récupération), tu commences sur le banc et tu entres entre la 58e et la 72e minute. Tes occasions arrivent après ton entrée.</dd></div><div><dt>Sorties et changements</dt><dd>Chaque équipe fait trois changements entre la 58e et la 84e minute : les remplaçants ont des jambes fraîches. Le coach te fait sortir si ton énergie tombe sous 30 %, ou en fin de match si tu n’as rien apporté (deux pertes, aucun but ni passe décisive) alors qu’il te fait peu confiance.</dd></div><div><dt>Scénarios particuliers</dt><dd>Certaines journées ont leur histoire : derby contre l’Étoile du Port (adversaire plus fort, XP ×1,2), remontada (tu entres à la 55e, mené 0–1), dernière journée (bonus en cas de victoire), match sous la pluie (contrôles et passes plus difficiles). Un carton rouge peut aussi laisser une équipe à dix.</dd></div><div><dt>Son du stade</dt><dd>Le public monte en intensité quand le ballon approche d’une surface, pendant tes occasions et dans les dernières minutes d’un match serré. Il explose sur un but, souffle sur une occasion manquée. Coupe le son avec 🔊 ou depuis l’accueil.</dd></div><div><dt>Célébrations</dt><dd>Ta célébration dépend du moment : saut pour ton premier but, doigts vers le ciel pour un doublé, triplé, glissade à genoux pour un but décisif en fin de match, salut aux supporters dans un derby, l’avion sinon. Après une égalisation, pas de fête : tu vas chercher le ballon au fond des filets. Tes partenaires viennent te rejoindre.</dd></div><div><dt>Replay</dt><dd>Après une action, la barre « REPLAY DISPONIBLE » remplace le curseur d’effet et montre le temps qu’il te reste. « Revoir l’action » ouvre le lecteur : lecture et pause, ±2 secondes, retour au début, barre de défilement et quatre caméras (derrière le but, tribune, joueur, aérienne). Tu peux le revoir autant que tu veux ; le match attend que tu fermes le lecteur.</dd></div><div><dt>Ton poste</dt><dd>Choisis-le dans Profil. Avant-centre : occasions dans la surface, centres et reprises. Ailier : un contre un sur ton aile, puis centre ou repique. Meneur : dernière passe devant la défense et frappes de loin.</dd></div><div><dt>Pressing</dt><dd>Parfois, un adversaire relance près de toi : touche l’herbe pour courir sur lui. Prends-lui le ballon au contact ou coupe sa passe ; s’il garde le ballon 6 secondes ou ressort proprement, le pressing est raté.</dd></div><div><dt>Contrôle à la réception</dt><dd>Quand une passe arrive, le jeu s’arrête au contact. Touche une direction pour orienter ton contrôle, touche ton joueur pour protéger, ou joue directement en une touche (frappe ou passe).</dd></div><div><dt>Appel après une passe</dt><dd>Pendant ta passe, touche ton joueur pour venir dans les pieds, ou touche plus loin pour partir en profondeur : la remise arrive dans ta course.</dd></div><div><dt>Passe dans l’espace</dt><dd>Pose le doigt sur un partenaire puis glisse vers une zone libre : il part dans l’espace et le ballon est dosé pour sa course.</dd></div><div><dt>Feinte de frappe</dt><dd>Tire vers toi comme pour frapper, puis relâche au-dessus de ton point de départ. Le défenseur peut se jeter… mais il mord de moins en moins si tu répètes.</dd></div><div><dt>Poussée de balle</dt><dd>Pendant un dribble, glisse vers l’avant : longue touche et sprint. Efficace dans l’espace, risqué si un défenseur est proche.</dd></div><div><dt>Protéger le ballon</dt><dd>Touche ton joueur (pas le ballon) : il se met dos au défenseur et un partenaire vient en soutien.</dd></div><div><dt>Pied faible</dt><dd>Ton joueur est droitier. Une frappe ou une passe qui part nettement vers sa gauche se joue du pied faible (« PIED FAIBLE ») : moins précise et moins forte. Il progresse à chaque utilisation, plus vite à l’entraînement.</dd></div><div><dt>Duel aérien</dt><dd>Pendant un centre, touche l’herbe pour te placer. Si un défenseur est mieux placé que toi sous le ballon, il gagne la tête.</dd></div><div><dt>Regarder autour</dt><dd>Le bouton 👁 élargit la vue sur tout le camp adverse ; touche-le de nouveau pour revenir près de ton joueur. Les partenaires hors de l’écran apparaissent en ronds bleus sur le bord : touche-en un pour lui passer le ballon.</dd></div><div><dt>Défenseurs : épaule et tacles</dt><dd>Un défenseur qui te rattrape court à ta hauteur, épaule contre épaule : tu ralentis et tu peux perdre le ballon. Certains tentent un tacle glissé : s’il te rate, il reste au sol une seconde, profites-en. S’il te touche avant le ballon, c’est une faute : coup franc là où elle a eu lieu, penalty dans la surface, carton jaune ou rouge (rouge s’il était le dernier défenseur).</dd></div><div><dt>Retournée</dt><dd>Sur un ballon en l’air à hauteur de poitrine ou de tête, glisse rapidement vers le haut (« RETOURNÉE ») : ton joueur se jette dos au but et frappe au-dessus de sa tête. Spectaculaire mais imprécis, et il reste au sol un instant.</dd></div><div><dt>Reprise et tête</dt><dd>Sur un centre, le jeu s’arrête quand le ballon arrive sur toi. Glisse vers toi pour reprendre de volée ou de la tête, ou touche l’herbe pour contrôler. Une reprise est plus difficile à placer qu’une frappe au sol.</dd></div><div><dt>Coups francs et penalties</dt><dd>Coup franc : le mur saute à la frappe, choisis ton effet ou sers un partenaire dans la surface. Penalty : seule la frappe est possible, le gardien choisit un côté au moment du tir.</dd></div><div><dt>Mettre de l’effet</dt><dd>Règle le curseur EFFET avant ta frappe, de 100 % à gauche à 100 % à droite. Avec de l’effet, la trajectoire en pointillés suit le ballon sur 40 % du chemin pour que tu voies la courbe.</dd></div><div><dt>Temps d’arrêt sur les passes</dt><dd>Quand une passe part (la tienne ou celle d’un partenaire), le jeu s’arrête jusqu’à 3 secondes : touche l’endroit où tu veux courir (un cercle bleu marque ta course), ou ton joueur pour rester.</dd></div><div><dt>Tourner la tête</dt><dd>Garde le doigt sur 👁 et glisse à gauche ou à droite : la caméra pivote autour de ton joueur. Un simple appui bascule la vue large.</dd></div></dl><p class="tiny">Chaque reprise de balle attend ton geste. Les défenseurs pressent pendant les actions, puis s’arrêtent quand tu dois choisir la suite. Ton énergie baisse avec les minutes et les efforts : elle influence tes courses et ta force. Récupère depuis l’accueil entre les matchs. Le jeu est en pause tant que cette aide est ouverte.</p><button id="guideClose" class="primary">À moi de jouer →</button></div></aside>
</section><section id="result" hidden><div class="card resultcard"><p class="eyebrow">COUP DE SIFFLET FINAL</p><h1 id="resultScore">2 — 1</h1><p id="resultFixture" class="muted"></p><div class="rating"><b id="rating">8,0</b><span>NOTE DU MATCH</span></div><h2 id="headline">Un début prometteur.</h2><p id="resultDetail" class="muted"></p><p id="resultEvents" class="tiny"></p><p id="resultScenario" class="tiny"></p><p id="resultCoach" class="tiny"></p><p id="resultEnergy" class="tiny"></p><p id="reward" class="reward"></p><button id="back" class="primary">Retour à ma carrière →</button></div></section><footer><span>NEXT NINE</span><span>Le terrain t’appartient.</span><span id="footBuild">Entre sur le terrain</span></footer></main><script type="module" src="game.js?v=20-4"></script></body></html>

===== dist/movement.js =====
import {clamp,GOAL_Z,G,R,PITCH} from './physics.js?v=20-4';
// V19 · On the real 105 m pitch everybody runs 35 % faster: real sprint speeds, the same balance between players.
export const PACE=1.35;
export function moveToward(actor,target,speed,dt,acceleration=12,brake=true){speed*=PACE;acceleration*=PACE;const dx=target.x-actor.x,dz=target.z-actor.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(d<.035){actor.speed=0;actor.vx=0;actor.vz=0;return true;}const desired=brake?Math.min(speed,Math.sqrt(16*d)):speed,tx=dx/d*desired,tz=dz/d*desired,cx=tx-(actor.vx||0),cz=tz-(actor.vz||0),change=Math.hypot(cx,cz)||1,gain=Math.min(1,acceleration*dt/change);actor.vx=(actor.vx||0)+cx*gain;actor.vz=(actor.vz||0)+cz*gain;const step=Math.hypot(actor.vx,actor.vz)*dt;if(step>=d&&actor.vx*dx+actor.vz*dz>0){actor.x=target.x;actor.z=target.z;actor.vx=actor.vz=actor.speed=0;return true;}actor.x+=actor.vx*dt;actor.z+=actor.vz*dt;actor.speed=Math.hypot(actor.vx,actor.vz);if(actor.speed>.03)actor.angle=Math.atan2(-actor.vx,-actor.vz);return false;}

export function dribbleTarget(start,target){const dx=target.x-start.x,dz=target.z-start.z,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,d=clamp(len,.25,5);return {x:clamp(start.x+dx/len*d,-PITCH.W+1,PITCH.W-1),z:clamp(start.z+dz/len*d,-19.35,PITCH.far-1)};}
export function createKeeper(){return {x:0,z:-19.25,y:0,vx:0,vz:0,speed:0,angle:Math.PI,state:'set',elapsed:0,shotTime:0,dir:0,tilt:0,reach:0,jump:0,diveStartX:0};}
export function updateKeeper(k,ball,dt,flying){k.elapsed+=dt;if(k.holding&&k.state==='set'){k.speed=0;return;}if(k.state==='set'){
 if(!k.rush){const ideal=clamp(ball.x*.24,-1.2,1.2);const old=k.x;k.x+=clamp(ideal-k.x,-1.8*dt,1.8*dt);k.speed=Math.abs(k.x-old)/dt;}
 if(flying&&ball.vz<-.2&&!k.noReact){if(!k.shotTime){k.react=.2+Math.random()*.16;/* Lot 4 · he reads the shot, he does not know it: the harder and further from him, the bigger his misjudgement */k.misread=(Math.random()-.5)*(.25+Math.hypot(ball.vx,ball.vz)*.03);}k.shotTime+=dt;if(k.shotTime>(k.react||.2)){const t=(GOAL_Z-ball.z)/ball.vz;const impact=ball.x+ball.vx*t+(k.misread||0)*Math.min(1,Math.abs(ball.x+ball.vx*t-k.x)/1.5);const height=ball.y+ball.vy*t-G*t*t/2;if(t>0&&t<.52&&Math.abs(impact)<4.8&&height<3.5){k.state='dive';k.elapsed=0;k.dir=Math.sign(impact-k.x)||1;k.diveStartX=k.x;k.diveDX=clamp(impact-k.x,-1.3,1.3);k.diveT=t;k.jump=Math.abs(k.diveDX)<.6?clamp(height-1.45,0,.85):clamp(height*.5,.2,1.2);}}}
 }else if(k.state==='dive'){
 const p=clamp(k.elapsed/.55,0,1);/* Lot 2 · the dive covers the distance to the ball: a shot at him is blocked standing, not dived away from */const dx=k.diveDX??k.dir*1.5,lat=Math.min(1,Math.abs(dx)/1.2);k.x=k.diveStartX+dx*Math.sin(p*Math.PI/2);k.tilt=k.dir*Math.min(p*2,1)*1.32*lat;const jp=clamp(k.elapsed/(2*(k.diveT||.28)),0,1);/* the jump peaks when the ball arrives */k.y=Math.sin(jp*Math.PI)*k.jump-p*.5*lat;k.reach=Math.sin(p*Math.PI/2)*.72*lat;k.speed=0;if(p>=1){k.state='ground';k.elapsed=0;}
 }else if(k.state==='ground'){k.speed=0;if(k.elapsed>.7){k.state='recover';k.elapsed=0;}}
 else if(k.state==='recover'){const p=clamp(k.elapsed/.8,0,1);k.tilt=k.dir*1.32*(1-p);k.y=-.5*(1-p);k.reach=.72*(1-p);if(p>=1){k.state='set';k.elapsed=0;k.shotTime=0;k.dir=0;}}
}
export function keeperTouches(ball,k){if(k.state==='ground')return Math.abs(ball.z-k.z)<.6&&Math.abs(ball.x-(k.x+k.dir*.45))<1&&ball.y<.65;if(k.state==='dive'){const handX=k.x+k.dir*k.reach;const handY=clamp(1.65+k.y-Math.abs(k.tilt)*.5,.5,2.6);if(Math.abs(k.diveDX??1)<.6&&Math.abs(ball.z-k.z)<.55&&Math.abs(ball.x-k.x)<.5+R&&ball.y<2.25+Math.max(0,k.y))return true;return Math.abs(ball.z-k.z)<.65&&Math.hypot(ball.x-handX,ball.y-handY)<.42+R;}
return Math.abs(ball.z-k.z)<.55&&Math.abs(ball.x-k.x)<.48+R&&ball.y<2.1;}
// Touchscreen slingshot: pulling down sends the aim upwards on screen.
export function slingPoint(start,current,anchor,gain=2.2){return {x:anchor.x+(start.x-current.x)*gain,y:anchor.y+(start.y-current.y)*gain};}

export function pressDefender(defender,carrier,ball,intent,dt,primary=true,waiting=false,cover=null,intensity=1){
 const dx=intent.x-carrier.x,dz=intent.z-carrier.z,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;
 let target=primary?{x:ball.x+dx/len*.35,z:ball.z+dz/len*.35}:cover?{x:ball.x*.45+cover.x*.55,z:ball.z*.45+cover.z*.55}:{x:carrier.x*.45+defender.home.x*.55,z:Math.max(-17,carrier.z-2.2)};
 if(waiting&&primary){const px=defender.x-carrier.x,pz=defender.z-carrier.z,d=Math.hypot(px,pz)||1;if(d<1.4){defender.speed=0;defender.vx=0;defender.vz=0;defender.angle=Math.atan2(-(carrier.x-defender.x),-(carrier.z-defender.z));return;}target={x:carrier.x+px/d*1.36,z:carrier.z+pz/d*1.36};}
 const speed=(waiting?primary?2.8:2.2:primary?3.9:3.3)*intensity;
 if(waiting){moveToward(defender,target,speed,dt);return;}
 const tx=target.x-defender.x,tz=target.z-defender.z,distance=Math.hypot(tx,tz)||1;
 const desiredX=tx/distance*speed,desiredZ=tz/distance*speed,changeX=desiredX-(defender.vx||0),changeZ=desiredZ-(defender.vz||0),change=Math.hypot(changeX,changeZ)||1,gain=Math.min(1,8*dt/change);
 defender.vx=(defender.vx||0)+changeX*gain;defender.vz=(defender.vz||0)+changeZ*gain;
 defender.x+=defender.vx*dt;defender.z+=defender.vz*dt;defender.speed=Math.hypot(defender.vx,defender.vz);if(defender.speed>.03)defender.angle=Math.atan2(-defender.vx,-defender.vz);
}
// Resolve cylinder contacts on the ground, keeping the controlled player stable.
export function separateBodies(a,b,min=.82,fixedB=false){
 let dx=a.x-b.x,dz=a.z-b.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(d>=min)return false;
 if(d<.00001){dx=1;dz=0;d=1;const shift=fixedB?min:min/2;a.x+=shift;if(!fixedB)b.x-=shift;return true;}
 const overlap=min-d,share=fixedB?1:.5;a.x+=dx/d*overlap*share;a.z+=dz/d*overlap*share;if(!fixedB){b.x-=dx/d*overlap*.5;b.z-=dz/d*overlap*.5;}return true;
}
export function keeperGrip(k){return {x:k.x+(k.state==='dive'||k.state==='ground'?k.dir*k.reach:0),y:Math.max(R,.92+k.y),z:k.z+.42};}
export function sweptKeeperTouch(before,after,k){
 const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(after.x-before.x,after.y-before.y,after.z-before.z)/.07));
 for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++){const t=i/steps,p={x:before.x+(after.x-before.x)*t,y:before.y+(after.y-before.y)*t,z:before.z+(after.z-before.z)*t};if(keeperTouches(p,k))return p;}return null;
}

const c0=(v,k)=>(v||0)*k;
export function beginChallenge(d,ball,kind='poke'){
 if(d.challenge||d.challengeCooldown>0)return false;
 const dx=ball.x-d.x,dz=ball.z-d.z,len=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;
 /* Lot 4 · a poke keeps the run going (a stab on the move), only a slide or a block plants the body */const carry=kind==='poke'?.8:0;d.challenge={kind,time:0,x:d.x,z:d.z,dx:dx/len,dz:dz/len,vx:(d.vx||0)*carry,vz:(d.vz||0)*carry};d.angle=Math.atan2(-dx,-dz);d.vx=c0(d.vx,carry);d.vz=c0(d.vz,carry);d.speed=Math.hypot(d.vx,d.vz);return true;
}
// V19 · Slide tackle: 2.6 m along the ground (decelerating), on the ground until 1.35 s, back up at 1.6 s.
export const SLIDE={length:2.6,slide:.55,down:1.35,up:1.6};
export function updateChallenge(d,dt){
 if(!d.challenge){d.challengeCooldown=Math.max(0,(d.challengeCooldown||0)-dt);return false;}
 const c=d.challenge;c.time+=dt;
 if(c.kind==='slide'){const u=Math.min(1,c.time/SLIDE.slide),e=1-(1-u)*(1-u);d.x=c.x+c.dx*SLIDE.length*e;d.z=c.z+c.dz*SLIDE.length*e;d.speed=0;if(c.time>=SLIDE.up){d.challenge=null;d.challengeCooldown=1.2;}return true;}const extend=c.time<.12?0:c.time<.32?(c.time-.12)/.2:c.time<.55?1-(c.time-.32)/.23:0;
 if(c.vx||c.vz){const k=Math.exp(-dt*1.6);c.vx*=k;c.vz*=k;c.x+=c.vx*dt;c.z+=c.vz*dt;}d.x=c.x+c.dx*.38*extend;d.z=c.z+c.dz*.38*extend;d.speed=Math.hypot(c.vx||0,c.vz||0);d.vx=c.vx||0;d.vz=c.vz||0;
 if(c.time>=.72){d.challenge=null;d.challengeCooldown=.45;}return true;
}
export function challengeHits(d,ball){
 const c=d.challenge;if(c?.kind==='slide'){if(c.time<.06||c.time>.5)return false;const fx=d.x+c.dx*.9,fz=d.z+c.dz*.9;return Math.hypot(ball.x-fx,ball.z-fz)<.45+R&&ball.y<.6;}
 if(!c||c.time<.14||c.time>.47)return false;
 const e=c.time<.32?(c.time-.12)/.2:Math.max(0,1-(c.time-.32)/.23);
 const foot={x:d.x+c.dx*.75*e,z:d.z+c.dz*.75*e,y:c.kind==='block'?.75:.28};
 return Math.hypot(ball.x-foot.x,ball.z-foot.z)<.3+R&&Math.abs(ball.y-foot.y)<.3+R;
}

===== dist/physics.js =====
export const G=9.81,R=.22,GOAL_Z=-20,GOAL_HALF=3.66,GOAL_HEIGHT=2.44;
export const clamp=(x,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
// V19 · Real pitch, 105 × 68 m: the attacked goal line is z=-20, the other one z=85. Boards stand 2.5 m outside
// the lines; the stands start just behind them.
export const PITCH={W:34,far:85,board:36.5,boardEnd:3.5};
// The ball rebounds on the advertising boards and dies in the stands instead of flying through them.
export function stadiumBounds(ball){const B=PITCH.board,lo=GOAL_Z-PITCH.boardEnd,hi=PITCH.far+PITCH.boardEnd,goalMouth=Math.abs(ball.x)<3.9&&ball.y<2.6;
 const hitX=Math.abs(ball.x)>B,hitZ=ball.z<lo&&!goalMouth||ball.z>hi;if(!hitX&&!hitZ)return false;
 if(ball.y<1){if(hitX){ball.x=Math.sign(ball.x)*B;ball.vx*=-.35;}if(hitZ){ball.z=ball.z<lo?lo:hi;ball.vz*=-.35;}ball.spin=0;return true;}
 // Above the boards: into the stands, where the ball is stopped by the seats.
 const deep=Math.abs(ball.x)>B+2.4||ball.z<lo-2.4||ball.z>hi+2.4;if(deep){ball.x=clamp(ball.x,-B-2.4,B+2.4);ball.z=clamp(ball.z,lo-2.4,hi+2.4);ball.vx*=.1;ball.vz*=.1;ball.vy=Math.min(ball.vy,0);ball.spin=0;}return true;}
export function launch(start,target,mode,spin=0,power=null,ability=1){
 if(mode==='normal'&&power!==null){const dx=target.x-start.x,dz=target.z-start.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,p=clamp(power,.08,1),speed=(9+22*p)*ability;return {x:start.x,y:R,z:start.z,vx:dx/d*speed,vz:dz/d*speed,vy:1.2+2.8*p+Math.max(0,p-.72)*32,bounces:0,age:0,spin:clamp(spin,-1,1)};}
 const distance=Math.hypot(target.x-start.x,target.z-start.z);
 const duration=mode==='low'?clamp(distance/21,.3,1.25):mode==='lob'?clamp(distance/13,1.05,2.2):clamp(distance/23,.38,1.55);
 // A ground pass rolls; the two aerial modes use a ballistic arc.
 const factor=mode==='low'?.55/(1-Math.exp(-.55*duration))*(1+(ability-1)*.75):1/duration;
 return {x:start.x,y:R,z:start.z,vx:(target.x-start.x)*factor,vy:mode==='low'?0:G*duration/2,vz:(target.z-start.z)*factor,bounces:0,age:0,spin:clamp(spin,-1,1)};
}
export function stepBall(ball,dt){const before={...ball};ball.age+=dt;
 if(ball.spin){const turn=ball.spin*.75*Math.min(1,Math.hypot(ball.vx,ball.vz)/20)*(ball.y>R+.02?1:.12)*dt,c=Math.cos(turn),s=Math.sin(turn),vx=ball.vx;ball.vx=vx*c-ball.vz*s;ball.vz=vx*s+ball.vz*c;ball.spin*=Math.exp(-.35*dt);}
 const g=ball.dip&&ball.vy<0&&ball.y>R+.05?G*1.9:G;ball.x+=ball.vx*dt;ball.z+=ball.vz*dt;ball.y+=ball.vy*dt-g*dt*dt/2;ball.vy-=g*dt;if(ball.y<=R+.02)ball.dip=false;if(ball.y<R){ball.y=R;if(ball.vy<-.7){ball.vy=-ball.vy*.42;ball.bounces++;}else ball.vy=0;ball.vx*=Math.exp(-.55*dt);ball.vz*=Math.exp(-.55*dt);}stadiumBounds(ball);return before;}
export function goalCrossing(before,after){if(before.z>GOAL_Z-R&&after.z<=GOAL_Z-R){const k=(before.z-(GOAL_Z-R))/(before.z-after.z);const x=before.x+(after.x-before.x)*k,y=before.y+(after.y-before.y)*k;return {x,y,inside:Math.abs(x)<GOAL_HALF-R&&y<GOAL_HEIGHT-R};}return null;}
export function hitPlayer(ball,p,radius=.55,height=1.8){return Math.hypot(ball.x-p.x,ball.z-p.z)<radius+R&&ball.y<height+R;}

// Closed scoring volume: after a valid goal, the ball belongs inside the net.
export function containGoalBall(ball){
 ball.x=clamp(ball.x,-GOAL_HALF+R,GOAL_HALF-R);
 ball.z=clamp(ball.z,GOAL_Z-2+R,GOAL_Z-R);
 ball.y=clamp(ball.y,R,GOAL_HEIGHT-R);
}
export function stepNetBall(ball,dt){
 stepBall(ball,dt);
 const left=-GOAL_HALF+R,right=GOAL_HALF-R,back=GOAL_Z-2+R,front=GOAL_Z-R,roof=GOAL_HEIGHT-R;
 if(ball.x<left||ball.x>right){ball.x=clamp(ball.x,left,right);ball.vx*=-.08;ball.netContact=true;}
 if(ball.z<back||ball.z>front){ball.z=clamp(ball.z,back,front);ball.vz*=-.06;ball.netContact=true;}
 if(ball.y>roof){ball.y=roof;ball.vy=-Math.abs(ball.vy)*.1;ball.netContact=true;}
 const drag=Math.exp(-(ball.netContact?6:1.5)*dt);ball.vx*=drag;ball.vz*=drag;
 if(Math.abs(ball.vx)<.025)ball.vx=0;if(Math.abs(ball.vz)<.025)ball.vz=0;
 if(ball.y<=R+.006&&Math.abs(ball.vy)<.3){ball.y=R;ball.vy=0;}
}

// Swept sphere against cylindrical posts and crossbar, in chronological order.
export function frameContact(before,ball){
 const n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(ball.x-before.x,ball.y-before.y,ball.z-before.z)/.04)),radius=R+.06;
 for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){const t=i/n,x=before.x+(ball.x-before.x)*t,y=before.y+(ball.y-before.y)*t,z=before.z+(ball.z-before.z)*t;
  const candidates=[];if(y<=GOAL_HEIGHT+.06)for(const px of [-GOAL_HALF,GOAL_HALF])candidates.push({x:px,y,z:GOAL_Z,kind:'post'});if(Math.abs(x)<=GOAL_HALF)candidates.push({x,y:GOAL_HEIGHT,z:GOAL_Z,kind:'bar'});
  for(const c of candidates){const dx=x-c.x,dy=y-c.y,dz=z-c.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz);if(d>radius||d<.00001)continue;const nx=dx/d,ny=dy/d,nz=dz/d,dot=ball.vx*nx+ball.vy*ny+ball.vz*nz;if(dot>=0)continue;ball.x=c.x+nx*(radius+.002);ball.y=Math.max(R,c.y+ny*(radius+.002));ball.z=c.z+nz*(radius+.002);ball.spin=0;ball.vx-=1.72*dot*nx;ball.vy-=1.72*dot*ny;ball.vz-=1.72*dot*nz;return c.kind;}
 }return null;
}

===== dist/tactical.js =====
import {clamp,PITCH,GOAL_Z} from './physics.js?v=20-4';
const formation=[[.5,.025],[.16,.25],[.39,.21],[.61,.21],[.84,.25],[.24,.44],[.5,.40],[.76,.44],[.17,.62],[.5,.66],[.83,.62]];
const dist=(a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y);
// V19 · The whole simulated pitch maps onto the real 105 × 68 m field.
const LEN=PITCH.far-GOAL_Z;
export const tacticalToWorld=p=>({x:(p.x-.5)*PITCH.W*2,z:PITCH.far-p.y*LEN});
export const worldToTactical=p=>({x:clamp(p.x/(PITCH.W*2)+.5,.015,.985),y:clamp((PITCH.far-p.z)/LEN,.008,.992)});
// 21 · The career player's position picks his dot: striker 9, right winger 10, playmaker 6.
export const HERO_INDEX={BU:9,AIL:10,MOC:6};
// 23 · Team tactics change the simulation itself: block height, pressing, ball-winning, passing choices and tempo.
export const TACTICS={equilibre:{label:'Équilibrée',line:0,press:1,win:1,keep:1,forward:2.8,safe:1,length:0,tempo:1},pressing:{label:'Pressing haut',line:.08,press:1.4,win:1.5,keep:1,forward:2.8,safe:1,length:0,tempo:.9},contre:{label:'Contre-attaque',line:-.09,press:.8,win:.85,keep:1.1,forward:4.6,safe:.8,length:.25,tempo:.55},possession:{label:'Possession',line:0,press:1.05,win:1.1,keep:.55,forward:1.7,safe:1.8,length:-.2,tempo:1.1}};
export function setTactic(s,id,team=0){s.tactics[team]=TACTICS[id]?id:'equilibre';}
// A high press fades after the hour: the legs go, the line stays high.
const tac=(s,team)=>{const t=TACTICS[s.tactics?.[team]]||TACTICS.equilibre;if(t.press<=1.2||s.minute<55)return t;const f=1-Math.min(1,(s.minute-55)/35)*.7;return {...t,press:1+(t.press-1)*f,win:1+(t.win-1)*f};};
export function createTactical(seed=1,options={}){let n=seed>>>0;const random=()=>{n=(1664525*n+1013904223)>>>0;return n/4294967296;};const position=HERO_INDEX[options.position]!=null?options.position:'BU',hero=HERO_INDEX[position],home=["Martin","Lopez","Morel","Diallo","Petit","Garcia","Leroy","Roux","Faye","Espoir","Blanc"];[home[9],home[hero]]=[home[hero],home[9]];const players=[];for(let team=0;team<2;team++)formation.forEach(([x,y],i)=>players.push({x,y:team?1-y:y,homeX:x,homeY:team?1-y:y,team,i,id:team*11+i,name:(team?["Bernard","Thomas","Robert","Dubois","Laurent","Michel","Simon","Durand","Adam","Henry","Moreau"]:home)[i]}));const entry=Math.max(0,options.entryMinute||0),start=clamp(options.startMinute||0,0,85);if(entry)players[hero].name='Lambert';const s={players,random,hero,position,entry,chanceFrom:Math.max(entry,start),rain:!!options.rain,onPitch:!entry,wantChances:options.allowChances!==false,tactics:[TACTICS[options.tactic]?options.tactic:'equilibre',TACTICS[options.opponentTactic]?options.opponentTactic:'equilibre'],won:[0,0],wonHigh:[0,0],passes:[0,0],offsides:[0,0],wonAt:[-99,-99],levels:options.levels||[60,60],energy:options.energy??100,elapsed:0,minute:start,decision:.5,pass:null,shot:null,comment:'Le coup d’envoi est donné.',event:'',goalHold:0,score:options.startScore?[...options.startScore]:[0,0],possession:[0,0],shots:[0,0],chances:0,lastChance:-8,paused:false,allowChances:options.allowChances!==false&&!entry};
 // 25 · Three changes per side between the 58th and 84th minute, drawn from a separate generator so the match itself is unchanged until then.
 let n2=(seed^0x5bd1e995)>>>0;const r2=()=>{n2=(1664525*n2+1013904223)>>>0;return n2/4294967296;};s.subPlan=[0,1].flatMap(team=>[0,1,2].map(()=>({team,minute:58+r2()*26,done:false})));s.bench=[['Caron','Mercier','Perrin'],['Fontaine','Girard','Bonnet']];
 // 26 · A rare red card (about one match in eleven) leaves one side with ten men.
 if(options.redCards&&r2()<.09)s.redPlan={minute:20+r2()*60,team:r2()<.5?0:1,pick:r2(),done:false};
 restartTactical(s,random()<.5?0:1);return s;}
// 25 · The coach takes the career player off: Lambert plays his position until the end, without chances.
export function heroOff(s){if(!s.onPitch)return;s.onPitch=false;s.left=true;s.allowChances=false;Object.assign(s.players[s.hero],{name:'Lambert',since:s.minute});s.comment='Changement : Espoir cède sa place à Lambert.';}
// Tired legs: up to 16 % slower at the end of the match, fresh substitutes run faster.
const legs=(s,p)=>1-.16*clamp((s.minute-(p.since||0))/90,0,1);
// A winger on his flank gets a one-against-one, a playmaker the last pass; a striker keeps the box chances.
export function chanceKindFor(s,p){return s.position==='AIL'&&Math.abs(p.x-.5)>.2?'wing':s.position==='MOC'?'playmaker':'open';}
// V19 · On the real pitch a chance starts 18–30 m from goal for a striker.
const OPEN_FROM={BU:.76,AIL:.72,MOC:.7};
export function restartTactical(s,team){for(const p of s.players){p.x=p.homeX;p.y=p.homeY;p.vx=p.vy=0;}s.owner=s.players[team*11+9];s.owner.x=.5;s.owner.y=.5;s.ball={x:.5,y:.5};s.pass=null;s.shot=null;s.decision=.4;s.paused=false;s.lastPasser=null;s.recent=[];}
export function tacticalGoal(s,team){s.pass=null;s.shot=null;s.goalHold=1.1;s.ball={x:.5,y:team===0?.997:.003};s.event=team===0?'BUT · AC RIVAGE':'BUT · ADVERSAIRE';s.comment=team===0?'Rivage trouve le chemin des filets !':'But adverse… Il faudra réagir.';s.restart=1-team;}
function lane(s,from,to,team){const dx=to.x-from.x,dy=to.y-from.y,ll=dx*dx+dy*dy;return s.players.filter(p=>p.team!==team&&p.i!==0&&!p.out).reduce((best,p)=>{const u=((p.x-from.x)*dx+(p.y-from.y)*dy)/(ll||1);if(u<.08||u>.95)return best;return Math.min(best,Math.hypot(p.x-from.x-u*dx,p.y-from.y-u*dy));},1);}
// V19 · Offside line: the second-last defender of the side defending against `team` (the keeper counts).
export function offsideLine(s,team){const dir=team===0?1:-1,ys=s.players.filter(p=>p.team!==team&&!p.out).map(p=>p.y).sort((a,b)=>(b-a)*dir);return ys[1]??(team===0?1:0);}
const isOffside=(s,p,from,team)=>{const dir=team===0?1:-1,line=offsideLine(s,team);return (p.y-line)*dir>.004&&(p.y-from.y)*dir>0&&(p.y-.5)*dir>0;};
function wonBall(s,p){s.won[p.team]++;s.wonAt[p.team]=s.elapsed;if(p.team===0?p.y>.5:p.y<.5)s.wonHigh[p.team]++;}
function move(p,t,speed,dt){const dx=t.x-p.x,dy=t.y-p.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,travel=Math.min(d,speed*dt);p.x=clamp(p.x+dx/d*travel,.015,.985);p.y=clamp(p.y+dy/d*travel,.015,.985);}
function choosePass(s){const o=s.owner,dir=o.team===0?1:-1,t=tac(s,o.team);const choices=s.players.filter(p=>p.team===o.team&&p!==o&&!p.out&&dist(p,o)>.07&&dist(p,o)<.65).map(p=>{const clear=lane(s,o,p,o.team);return {p,clear,score:(p.y-o.y)*dir*t.forward+Math.min(clear,.12)*6*t.safe-dist(p,o)*(.4-t.length)+(p.i===s.hero&&o.team===0?.25:0)-(p===s.lastPasser?.35:0)-(isOffside(s,p,o,o.team)?.6:0)+(s.random()-.5)*.2};}).filter(c=>c.clear>.022*t.safe).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);return choices[0]?.p;}
export function resumeTactical(s,actors,outcome,ball,ownerId){for(const a of actors){const p=s.players.find(p=>p.id===a.tacticalId);if(p)Object.assign(p,worldToTactical(a));}s.paused=false;s.pass=null;s.shot=null;s.event='';s.lastChance=s.minute;s.decision=.45;if(outcome==='goal'){s.score[0]++;tacticalGoal(s,0);return;}s.owner=s.players.find(p=>p.id===ownerId)||s.players[11];s.ball=worldToTactical(ball);if(outcome==='save'||outcome==='miss'){s.ball={x:s.owner.x,y:s.owner.y};s.comment='Le gardien relance. Le match continue.';}else{s.owner.x=s.ball.x;s.owner.y=s.ball.y;s.comment='Ballon récupéré : attention à la transition adverse.';}}
// V14: a chance carries its kind and the player who serves the ball, so the 3D action
// starts from the real passer (pass, cross) or from a set piece (free kick, penalty).
function chanceEvent(s,kind,passer){const hero=s.players[s.hero];
 // V19 · Open play: if the player is still far from goal, he has made a run into his zone before the ball arrives.
 if(['open','wing','playmaker'].includes(kind)){const want=OPEN_FROM[s.position]+.02;if(hero.y<want)hero.y=want+s.random()*.03;}s.paused=true;s.chances++;s.lastChance=s.minute;s.comment=kind==='penalty'?'Faute dans la surface : penalty !':kind==='freekick'?'Faute ! Coup franc bien placé.':kind==='cross'?'Le centre arrive !':kind==='wing'?'Espoir est lancé sur son aile !':kind==='playmaker'?'Espoir lève la tête au milieu…':'Une ouverture ! À toi de jouer.';const from=passer&&passer!==hero&&passer.team===0?passer:null;return {type:'chance',kind,minute:s.minute,heroId:hero.id,passerId:from?.id??null,passer:from?{x:from.x,y:from.y}:null,ball:{...s.ball},hero:{x:hero.x,y:hero.y}};}
// Minimum pace of playable chances: about one every 25 minutes, at least three per match.
export const chancesDue=(minute,entry=0)=>Math.min(3,Math.floor((minute-entry+14)/25));
export function stepTactical(s,dt){if(s.paused)return null;s.elapsed+=dt;
 if(s.goalHold>0){s.goalHold-=dt;if(s.goalHold<=0){s.event='';restartTactical(s,s.restart);}return null;}
 s.minute=Math.min(90,s.minute+dt*1.05);if(s.minute>=90){s.paused=true;return {type:'finish'};}
 // 24 · A substitute career player comes on at the planned minute; Lambert, who played his position, goes off.
 const red=s.redPlan;if(red&&!red.done&&s.minute>=red.minute){red.done=true;const list=s.players.filter(p=>p.team===red.team&&p.i>=1&&p.i<=7&&p!==s.owner&&p!==s.pass?.to&&!(p.team===0&&p.i===s.hero));const p=list[Math.floor(red.pick*list.length)];if(p){p.out=true;s.comment=`Carton rouge pour ${p.name} ! ${red.team?'L’adversaire':'Rivage'} à dix.`;return {type:'red',team:red.team,name:p.name,minute:s.minute};}}
 const plan=s.subPlan?.find(p=>!p.done&&s.minute>=p.minute);if(plan){plan.done=true;const out=s.players.filter(p=>p.team===plan.team&&p.i>0&&!(p.team===0&&p.i===s.hero)&&p!==s.owner&&p!==s.pass?.to&&p.since==null&&!p.out).sort((a,b)=>b.i-a.i)[0];if(out&&s.bench[plan.team].length){const was=out.name;out.name=s.bench[plan.team].shift();out.since=s.minute;s.comment=`Changement ${plan.team?'adverse':'chez Rivage'} : ${out.name} remplace ${was}.`;return {type:'change',team:plan.team,out:was,in:out.name,minute:s.minute};}}
 if(!s.onPitch&&s.entry&&!s.left&&s.minute>=s.entry){s.onPitch=true;s.allowChances=s.wantChances;Object.assign(s.players[s.hero],{name:'Espoir',since:s.minute});s.lastChance=s.minute-3;s.comment='Changement : Espoir entre en jeu !';return {type:'sub',dir:'in',minute:s.minute};}
 const o=s.owner,team=o.team,dir=team===0?1:-1,quality=clamp((s.levels[team]-s.levels[1-team])/40,-.65,.65);s.possession[team]+=dt;
 const opponents=s.players.filter(p=>p.team!==team&&p.i!==0&&!p.out),presser=opponents.reduce((best,p)=>dist(p,o)<dist(best,o)?p:best,opponents[0]),mine=tac(s,team),theirs=tac(s,1-team);
 // A pressing team sends a second player at the carrier.
 const presser2=theirs.press>1.2&&(team===0?o.y:1-o.y)<.5?opponents.filter(p=>p!==presser).reduce((best,p)=>!best||dist(p,o)<dist(best,o)?p:best,null):null;
 for(const p of s.players){if(p===o||p.out)continue;const attacking=p.team===team,pdir=p.team===0?1:-1;let tx=p.homeX+(s.ball.x-.5)*.20,ty=p.homeY;
  if(p.i===0){tx=.5+(s.ball.x-.5)*.12;ty=p.homeY;}
  else if(attacking){const progress=team===0?s.ball.y:1-s.ball.y;ty=p.homeY+pdir*clamp((progress-.42)*.65,-.15,.25);if(p.i>=8)ty=clamp(o.y+dir*((p.i===9?.13:.06)+mine.length*.12),.08,.92);const line=offsideLine(s,team);if(p.i>=8&&s.random()<dt*(p.early?.9:.08))p.early=!p.early;if(p.i>=8&&p.early&&(o.y-.5)*dir>-.05)ty=line+dir*.03;else ty=dir>0?Math.min(ty,line-.004):Math.max(ty,line+.004);}
  else{ty=p.homeY+clamp((s.ball.y-.5)*.36,-.20,.20)+pdir*theirs.line;if(p===presser){tx=o.x;ty=o.y-dir*.025;}else if(p===presser2){tx=(tx+o.x)/2;ty=(ty+o.y)/2;}else if(p.i<=4){const rival=s.players[team*11+(8+(p.i%3))];tx=tx*.65+rival.x*.35;}}
  if(s.pass?.to===p){tx=s.pass.target.x;ty=s.pass.target.y;}
  move(p,{x:clamp(tx,.05,.95),y:clamp(ty,.025,.975)},(p===presser?.065*(1-quality*.25)*theirs.press:.075)*legs(s,p),dt);
 }
 // Avoid piles of dots without moving the ball carrier or a pass destination.
 for(let i=0;i<s.players.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<s.players.length;j++){const a=s.players[i],b=s.players[j],d=dist(a,b);if(a.out||b.out)continue;if(d<.024&&d>1e-6){const push=(.024-d)*.25,dx=(a.x-b.x)/d,dy=(a.y-b.y)/d;if(a!==o){a.x=clamp(a.x+dx*push,.015,.985);a.y=clamp(a.y+dy*push,.015,.985);}if(b!==o){b.x=clamp(b.x-dx*push,.015,.985);b.y=clamp(b.y-dy*push,.015,.985);}}}
 if(s.shot){const f=s.shot;f.elapsed+=dt;const u=clamp(f.elapsed/f.duration,0,1);s.ball={x:f.from.x+(f.target.x-f.from.x)*u,y:f.from.y+(f.target.y-f.from.y)*u};if(u<1)return null;s.shot=null;const goal=s.random()<f.probability;if(goal){s.score[team]++;tacticalGoal(s,team);return {type:'goal',team,minute:s.minute,scorer:o.name||'Le numéro '+(o.i+1)};}s.owner=s.players[(1-team)*11];s.ball={x:s.owner.x,y:s.owner.y};s.decision=.5;s.comment='Arrêt du gardien ! Il prépare sa relance.';s.event='ARRÊT';return {type:'save',team,minute:s.minute};}
 if(s.pass){const f=s.pass;f.elapsed+=dt;const u=clamp(f.elapsed/f.duration,0,1);const before=s.ball;s.ball={x:f.from.x+(f.target.x-f.from.x)*u,y:f.from.y+(f.target.y-f.from.y)*u};const dx=s.ball.x-before.x,dy=s.ball.y-before.y,ll=dx*dx+dy*dy;const hit=opponents.find(p=>{const t=clamp(((p.x-before.x)*dx+(p.y-before.y)*dy)/(ll||1),0,1);return Math.hypot(p.x-before.x-t*dx,p.y-before.y-t*dy)<.014*(1-quality*.3)*(s.rain?1.12:1);});if(hit){wonBall(s,hit);s.owner=hit;s.ball={x:hit.x,y:hit.y};s.pass=null;s.decision=.25;s.comment='Interception ! Le jeu change de sens.';s.lastPasser=null;}else if(u>=1&&f.offside){const opp=s.players.filter(p=>p.team!==team&&!p.out).reduce((b,p)=>dist(p,f.target)<dist(b,f.target)?p:b);s.offsides[team]++;s.pass=null;s.owner=opp;s.ball={x:opp.x,y:opp.y};s.decision=.5;s.lastPasser=null;s.comment=`Hors-jeu de ${f.to.name} : coup franc ${team?'pour Rivage':'adverse'}.`;s.event='HORS-JEU';return {type:'offside',team,minute:s.minute,name:f.to.name};}
 else if(u>=1){s.passes[team]++;if(theirs.line>.04&&(f.target.y-f.from.y)*dir>.1)s.breakAt=[team,s.elapsed];s.lastPasser=o;s.owner=f.to;s.owner.x=f.target.x;s.owner.y=f.target.y;s.pass=null;s.decision=(.18+s.random()*.32)*mine.tempo;}return null;}
 const progress=team===0?o.y:1-o.y;s.event='';move(o,{x:clamp(o.x+(.5-o.x)*.15,.06,.94),y:o.y+dir*.15},.065*(1+quality*.3)*(1+mine.length)*(s.breakAt?.[0]===team&&s.elapsed-s.breakAt[1]<3.5?1.8:1)*legs(s,o),dt);s.ball={x:o.x,y:clamp(o.y+dir*.008,.005,.995)};
 // Fouls on Rivage carriers near the box: free kicks, and penalties when it happens centrally close to goal.
 if(team===0&&s.allowChances&&progress>.56&&s.minute-s.lastChance>3&&dist(presser,o)<.06&&s.random()<dt*.3){const box=progress>.66&&Math.abs(o.x-.5)<.24&&s.random()<.6;return chanceEvent(s,box?'penalty':'freekick',o);}
 if(dist(presser,o)<.028&&s.random()<dt*.8*(1-quality)*theirs.win*mine.keep){wonBall(s,presser);s.owner=presser;s.ball={x:presser.x,y:presser.y};s.decision=.25;s.comment='Le pressing permet de récupérer le ballon.';return null;}
 s.decision-=dt;if(s.decision>0)return null;
 // The career striker never finishes alone in the simulation: a shooting position becomes a playable chance.
 const behind=s.allowChances&&s.chances<chancesDue(s.minute,s.chanceFrom)&&s.minute-s.lastChance>2;
 if(team===0&&o.i===s.hero&&s.allowChances&&(progress>OPEN_FROM[s.position]&&(s.minute-s.lastChance>5&&s.random()<.3+s.energy/300||progress>.8&&s.minute-s.lastChance>2)||behind&&progress>OPEN_FROM[s.position]-.08))return chanceEvent(s,chanceKindFor(s,o),null);
 // 12 · An opposing defender building from the back near the career striker: a pressing chance.
 if(team===1&&o.i>=1&&o.i<=7&&s.allowChances&&progress<.45&&s.minute-s.lastChance>5&&dist(o,s.players[s.hero])<.2&&s.random()<.2){const e=chanceEvent(s,'press',null);e.carrierId=o.id;return e;}
 // Wide Rivage players in the final third can cross for the career striker (a winger attacks the far post; a playmaker is not in the box).
 if(team===0&&o.i!==s.hero&&s.allowChances&&s.position!=='MOC'&&(s.position!=='AIL'||(o.x-.5)*(s.players[s.hero].x-.5)<0)&&progress>.62&&Math.abs(o.x-.5)>.24&&s.minute-s.lastChance>6&&s.random()<.22)return chanceEvent(s,'cross',o);
 // Behind schedule: a Rivage attack in the opposing half is turned into a ball for the player.
 if(team===0&&o.i!==s.hero&&o.i>=5&&behind&&progress>(s.entry?.45:.52)){const wide=Math.abs(o.x-.5)>.27&&progress>.6;return chanceEvent(s,s.position!=='BU'?chanceKindFor(s,s.players[s.hero]):wide&&s.random()<.3?'cross':'open',o);}
 const heroDot=team===0&&o.i===s.hero&&s.allowChances;
 // Against a deep block, the attackers try their luck from further out (lower quality).
 if(!heroDot&&progress>.80+Math.min(0,theirs.line)*1.1&&Math.abs(o.x-.5)<.36){s.shots[team]++;s.shot={from:{...s.ball},target:{x:.5+(s.random()-.5)*.13,y:team===0?1:0},elapsed:0,duration:.65,probability:clamp(.15+quality*.17+(progress-.8)*.8+theirs.line*1.6-(theirs.keep<.7?.05:0)+(s.elapsed-s.wonAt[team]<7?.06*(1+mine.length*4):0),.06,.55)};s.comment=team===0?'Rivage frappe !':'Attention, frappe adverse !';s.event='TIR';return {type:'shot',team,minute:s.minute};}
 const to=choosePass(s);if(to&&team===0&&to.i===s.hero&&s.allowChances&&to.y>OPEN_FROM[s.position]-.05&&s.minute-s.lastChance>5&&s.random()<.4)return chanceEvent(s,s.position!=='BU'?chanceKindFor(s,to):Math.abs(o.x-.5)>.24&&to.y>.7&&Math.abs(to.x-.5)<.2?'cross':'open',o);if(to){const spread=.015*(1-quality)*(s.rain?1.5:1),target={x:clamp(to.x+(s.random()-.5)*spread,.04,.96),y:clamp(to.y+(s.random()-.5)*spread,.04,.96)};s.pass={from:{...s.ball},to,target,elapsed:0,duration:.25+dist(o,to)*1.1,offside:isOffside(s,to,o,team)||!!to.early&&(to.y-o.y)*dir>.04&&(to.y-.5)*dir>0};s.comment=team===1&&progress>.6?'L’adversaire se rapproche de notre surface…':team===0&&progress>.55?'Rivage avance : une ouverture peut se présenter.':Math.abs(to.x-o.x)>.30?'Renversement de jeu vers l’autre aile.':'Le ballon circule au milieu de terrain.';}s.decision=(.3+s.random()*.4)*mine.tempo;return null;
}

export function drawTactical(canvas,s){const ctx=canvas.getContext?.('2d');if(!ctx)return;const w=canvas.clientWidth||390,h=canvas.clientHeight||700,ratio=Math.min(globalThis.devicePixelRatio||1,2);if(canvas.width!==w*ratio||canvas.height!==h*ratio){canvas.width=w*ratio;canvas.height=h*ratio;}ctx.setTransform(ratio,0,0,ratio,0,0);ctx.fillStyle='#0c211c';ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);const ph=Math.max(100,Math.min(h-330,(w-40)*1.55)),pw=ph/1.55,x=(w-pw)/2,y=Math.max(110,135+(h-330-ph)/2);ctx.fillStyle='#245c40';ctx.fillRect(x,y,pw,ph);for(let i=0;i<10;i++){if(i%2===0){ctx.fillStyle='#286548';ctx.fillRect(x,y+i*ph/10,pw,ph/10);}}ctx.strokeStyle='#8ba99a';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(x,y,pw,ph);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y+ph/2);ctx.lineTo(x+pw,y+ph/2);ctx.moveTo(x+pw/2+pw*.14,y+ph/2);ctx.arc(x+pw/2,y+ph/2,pw*.14,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();for(const bottom of [false,true]){ctx.strokeRect(x+pw*.2,bottom?y+ph-ph*.16:y,pw*.6,ph*.16);ctx.strokeRect(x+pw*.41,bottom?y+ph:y-5,pw*.18,5);}for(const p of s.players){if(p.out)continue;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x+p.x*pw,y+(1-p.y)*ph,Math.max(3.5,pw*.017),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle=p.i===0?'#e6ce76':p.team===0?'#6ac9ff':'#fa927d';ctx.fill();if(p.team===0&&p.i===s.hero&&s.onPitch!==false){ctx.strokeStyle='#d3f96a';ctx.lineWidth=2.5;ctx.stroke();}ctx.strokeStyle='#102d27';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.stroke();}ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x+s.ball.x*pw,y+(1-s.ball.y)*ph,3,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fill();ctx.font='600 11px sans-serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillStyle='#b8d1c4';ctx.fillText('RIVAGE  ●    ·    ●  ADVERSAIRE',w/2,y+ph+24);if(s.event){ctx.fillStyle='#d3f96a';ctx.font='bold 18px sans-serif';ctx.fillText(s.event,w/2,y+ph/2);}}
